## Reverse Coverage Rule

- `explicit`: public symbol is explicitly named by a matrix row.
- `family-only`: only the parent API family/type is named; this is still treated as a matrix refinement gap for method/field-level docs.
- `missing`: no matching matrix row was found; symbol must be added or deliberately grouped before public developer docs claim full coverage.
- `MatrixGap` result means the review found a public Abstractions symbol without sufficient matrix-level enumeration.
